# Diffusion Planner CAMP Integration V14 Audit

Date: 2026-07-02

This file is the current short-form audit entry point for the CAMP integration
with the fixed TiERIV Diffusion Planner. It replaces v13 for new current-state
writes because v13 had grown too large to remain a clear operational source of
truth. The v13 audit remains historical evidence and the rollover source.

## Current V14 Approved Source Manifest Remediation Validated Rejected No Nonfixture DP-Native Source After 040f1f0

Executed the EOF-authorized approved source manifest remediation on AutoDL. The
first broad scan found one DP-core-key `.npz` and generated a manifest, but the
only file was the DP repository test fixture
`Diffusion-Planner/scenario_generation/tests/test_data/fixture_scene.npz`.
The final validated scan therefore rejects that manifest and records that no
valid nonfixture DP-native source `.npz` or approved manifest is available.

No fixed DP candidate generation, CAMP candidate generation, CAMP training, DP
modification, promotion, or deployment action was executed.

## Current V14 Source Data Availability Audit Rejected Missing Raw DP Source After 6f5bf60

Executed the current v14 next-work target by checking whether AutoDL has raw
source assets that can produce a valid nonfixture DP-native `.npz` manifest.
Diffusion Planner documents the standard data path as rosbag directories plus
maps through `ros_scripts/parse_rosbag_for_directory.py`, followed by
`diffusion_planner/util_scripts/create_train_set_path.py`. AutoDL currently has
maps and route pickles, but no bag metadata, rosbag `.db3`, `.mcap`, or usable
C++ training binary sources.

DP's `scenario_generation.replay` can dump per-step observation NPZs, but those
are closed-loop replay observations and remain disallowed as CAMP training or
online input under the current boundary. Synthetic static source generation is
also not authorized by the current EOF. No fixed DP candidate generation, CAMP
training, DP modification, promotion, or deployment action was executed.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Source Reclassification Unblocked Candidate Tensor Preflight After 88fd3ca

The previous EOF remains valid only for the narrow TIER IV DP training-source
question: AutoDL does not currently contain a nonfixture TIER IV DP training
source `.npz`, Autoware rosbag metadata, `.db3`, `.mcap`, or C++ training binary
source that can reproduce TIER IV's official rosbag-to-DP-`.npz` training-data
path. NuScenes is present.

AutoDL does expose public nuScenes archives under `/autodl-pub/data/nuScenes`.
Those archives are a real available data source, but they are not currently
extracted into CAMP data paths, converted to DP-native records, or part of the
official TIER IV rosbag-to-DP-`.npz` converter path. A nuScenes-to-DP adapter
would be a separate data-adapter gate and is not authorized by this EOF.

The current objective is not to train or modify Diffusion Planner. CAMP must
only rerank/select fixed candidate tensors generated by the unmodified fixed
TIER IV DP checkpoint. TIER IV's public guidance provides the correct public
route for that objective: use the official v5.0 DP weights and parameter file
with public Autoware planning-simulator maps and declared routes. The official
TIER IV answer states that published DP weights used internal data and points
to the rosbag converter for DP training data; the Autoware planning-simulation
docs provide the public sample map, model-artifact workflow, simulator launch,
and Nishishinjuku lane-change map/scenario flow.

Therefore the external DP-native source `.npz` manifest gate is reclassified
as too strict for CAMP fixed-DP candidate tensor training. The new next gate is
a preflight for public simulator fixed-DP candidate generation. No candidate
generation, CAMP training, DP modification, promotion, deployment, online
selector change, or safety-benefit claim was executed by this reclassification.

Remote evidence was collected on AutoDL after local and `origin/main` were both
at `88fd3cac6722aedfd4ca13b41f904b4a3331c219`. AutoDL CAMP matched
`origin/main` with unrelated untracked files only. AutoDL DP remained fixed at
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Fixed-DP Candidate Generation Preflight Ready After 1ffff59

The v14 public simulator fixed-DP candidate generation preflight was executed
on AutoDL after the preflight implementation and direct tests were committed
and synchronized at `1ffff597ebdc0cc598daff7db2150df2d5d898ab`.

This gate only validated the public simulator inputs, fixed DP head, CAMP head,
static default-off shadow reranker artifacts, and a guarded execution runbook.
It did not execute the runbook, generate candidates, train CAMP, modify DP,
promote a selector or atom, deploy anything, change the online selector, or
make any safety-benefit or CAMP-over-DP Top-1 claim.

The planned execution remains fixed-DP candidate generation from the public
simulator source. CAMP remains a default-off shadow reranker/selector over the
fixed DP candidate tensor. The executed trajectory policy in the runbook is
DP Top-1; CAMP only logs shadow selected indices and candidate tensor
provenance. The score contract remains affine:
`score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, with only approved atom features and nonnegative simplex
weights allowed by later CAMP training gates.

The guarded runbook requires
`DP_CAMP_V14_PUBLIC_SIMULATOR_FIXED_DP_CANDIDATE_GENERATION_EXECUTE=1`,
rechecks CAMP HEAD, rechecks the fixed DP HEAD, and refuses to run if the
candidate output root already exists.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Fixed-DP Candidate Generation Execution Passed After 458c66c

The v14 public simulator fixed-DP candidate generation execution gate ran on
AutoDL under the explicit
`DP_CAMP_V14_PUBLIC_SIMULATOR_FIXED_DP_CANDIDATE_GENERATION_EXECUTE=1` guard.
The execution used the preflight-approved command matrix from
`/root/autodl-tmp/camp_dp_v14_public_simulator_fixed_dp_candidate_generation_preflight_1ffff597eb_20260702T172252CST`
and the CAMP execution wrapper committed at
`458c66c8aeac8b9eb15ba3f06a7f87e5c9ef0740`.

This execution generated fixed Diffusion Planner candidate replay artifacts
from the public simulator routes. CAMP did not generate, repair, blend, or
rewrite trajectories. The CAMP path remained default-off shadow reranking over
fixed DP candidate tensors with candidate tensor provenance logging. The
executed trajectory policy remained DP Top-1. No CAMP training, data
preparation, selector/atom promotion, deployment, online selector change, DP
modification, safety-benefit claim, or CAMP-over-DP Top-1 claim was executed.

The original wrapper `SHA256SUMS` was produced before the outer shell wrote the
final stdout JSON and exit marker, so a post-execution SHA manifest was added

wrapper exit, and all command logs. The wrapper implementation was then patched
to exclude externally mutated wrapper logs from its internal `SHA256SUMS` in
future execution gates.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Fixed-DP Candidate Generation Zero-Overlap Validation Passed After 2e17d11

The v14 zero-overlap validation gate was run on AutoDL against the fixed DP
candidate replay artifacts generated by the public simulator execution. This
gate did not replay, generate new candidates, train CAMP, modify DP, change the
online selector, promote, deploy, or make a safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP Top-1
claim. CAMP remained a default-off shadow reranker over the fixed DP candidate
tensor, and the executed trajectory remained DP Top-1.

The first validation attempt used an incomplete historical reference registry
root. It correctly rejected the artifact because three required reference
registry classes were empty, even though the new v14 generated material had no
observed intersections. That rejected artifact is evidence of reference-root
incompleteness only and is not a passing holdout claim.

The second validation attempt used the complete historical reference registry
root and passed with zero intersections for all required keys:
`candidate_tensor_hash`, `path_signature`, `record_identity_hash`, and
`split_manifest_root`.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Fixed-DP Candidate Data-Preparation Preflight Ready After 356ce63

The v14 public simulator fixed-DP candidate data-preparation preflight ran on
AutoDL against the zero-overlap fixed DP candidate replay outputs. The preflight
validated the existing 32 selection logs and 3200 records against the clean
DP-native training-data contract, wrote a training input manifest, and did not
run replay, generate candidates, prepare training arrays, train CAMP, modify DP,
change the online selector, promote, deploy, or make a safety-benefit/CAMP-over-
DP Top-1 claim.

The candidate source remains the fixed DP candidate tensor output produced by
Diffusion Planner. CAMP remains limited to a default-off shadow reranker over
that tensor. The score remains affine, `score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, and the preflight
keeps approved atoms on nonnegative simplex weights with simplex/CVaR/L2 master
convexity preserved.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Fixed-DP Candidate Training Preflight Ready After aff9b05

The v14 public simulator fixed-DP candidate CAMP training preflight ran on
AutoDL against the data-preparation training input manifest. The preflight
validated the 32 fixed-DP selection logs and 3200 records, wrote a selection
manifest, and wrote a guarded training command plan for the next gate. It did
not train CAMP, run replay, generate candidates, modify DP, change the online
selector, promote, deploy, or make a safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP claim.

The planned training input remains only the fixed Diffusion Planner candidate
tensor. CAMP remains limited to an affine reranker over approved atom features:
`score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, with nonnegative simplex weights and convex
simplex/CVaR/L2 master assumptions preserved. Records with no feasible
candidate are explicitly counted as dropped by static training rather than
treated as closed-loop outcomes.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Fixed-DP Candidate Training Execution Passed After 67f806

The v14 public simulator fixed-DP candidate CAMP training execution ran on
AutoDL from the training-preflight command plan. The execution trained static
nonnegative simplex CAMP weights over the fixed Diffusion Planner candidate
tensor atom features and wrote the offline weights, atom scales, and training
summary. It did not run replay, generate candidates, modify DP, change the
online selector, promote, deploy, or make a safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP claim.

This checkpoint is a training artifact only. It is not a deployable checkpoint
claim and it is not a selector promotion. The next gate must review the
training artifact contract before any later shadow replay/evaluation gate.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Trained Default-Off Shadow Replay/Evaluation Preflight Ready After adc714

The v14 trained default-off shadow replay/evaluation preflight ran on AutoDL
after adding the preflight script and fixing direct script import-path handling.
The first AutoDL attempt at `bedc10752b202605c149100ab6cd3a4e6aee118b`
failed before contract evaluation because the script was launched without
`PYTHONPATH` and could not import `camp_core`. Commit
`adc71422af56711f8baec545259fe47626f955ef` fixed that by injecting the repo
and package paths in the script itself. The passing preflight then emitted a
guarded runbook and runtime manifest for a future trained default-off shadow
replay/evaluation execution. It did not run replay, generate candidates, train
CAMP, modify DP, change the online selector, promote, deploy, or make a
safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP claim.

The runtime manifest pins the trained static CAMP weights and atom scales from
the reviewed training artifact. The planned commands keep CAMP default-off:
CAMP may compute and log a shadow selected index, but the executed trajectory
policy remains DP Top-1. The score remains affine,
`score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, over the approved nine `camp_legacy_v1_9d` atoms with
nonnegative simplex weights. No closed-loop outcome labels, formal seeds
11/12/13, guidance, reference blending, postprocessing, or postselection are
authorized by this gate.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Trained Default-Off Shadow Replay/Evaluation Execution Passed After 72fdb3

The first execution attempt used the earlier `adc71422` preflight runbook after
the audit commit had moved CAMP to `5b23ae8f25faa84d0d74b5e4e8fdf2075e2a9259`.
It failed closed before replay with exit `41` and `CAMP HEAD mismatch`. That
failure is stale-runbook evidence, not an evaluation result. Commit
`72fdb3e4c880751948a47d25b0330e3818975162` added the minimal preflight
parameterization needed to refresh the guarded runbook at the current source
HEAD. The refreshed preflight passed, and the current-head execution then
completed on AutoDL with CAMP, GitHub `origin/main`, and AutoDL CAMP all pinned
to `72fdb3e4c880751948a47d25b0330e3818975162`. DP stayed fixed at
`7a1d33da277a1992ec474b5383a0c963c72e04e4`.

The execution is a default-off shadow replay/evaluation result only. CAMP
computed shadow scores and shadow selected indices over the fixed DP candidate
tensor; the executed output stayed DP Top-1 for every record. The selected
online trajectory did not change. This section does not promote the selector,
does not deploy a checkpoint, and does not claim safety benefit or CAMP
superiority over DP Top-1.

## Current V14 Public Simulator Trained Default-Off Shadow Replay/Evaluation Result Review Passed After 2dd27b

The first result-review attempt at
`3642c74a10dab07fd141161a11507a01404f47dd` rejected because the review script
incorrectly required the source execution artifact HEAD to match the newer
review script HEAD. That was too strict for append-only gates: the execution
artifact was correctly produced at
`72fdb3e4c880751948a47d25b0330e3818975162`, while the result-review script was
added later. Commit `2dd27b50b8172fb6f31df9a154e55c329f6ae2f9` changed the
contract to require the artifact HEAD to match the execution HEAD already
recorded in this v14 audit. The corrected result review then passed on AutoDL.

This result review is read-only. It did not run replay, generate candidates,
train CAMP, modify DP, change executed trajectory selection, promote a selector
or atom, deploy, or make safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP claims. Passing this gate
only authorizes a future promotion-decision plan gate; it does not authorize
promotion itself.

## Current V14 Trained Shadow Replay/Evaluation Promotion-Decision Plan Ready After 4b17b3

The promotion-decision planning gate was executed on AutoDL after explicit user
authorization. The gate consumed the existing trained default-off shadow
replay/evaluation result review and emitted a planning artifact only. It did
not promote, deploy, train CAMP, run replay, generate candidates, modify DP,
change online selection, or make safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP claims.

The first runbook attempt is retained as evidence. It failed before executing
the planning script because the AutoDL shell did not expose a `python` alias.
The rerun used `/root/miniconda3/bin/python`, passed the plan-only script, and
kept the current DP head fixed.

The decision is conservative: do not promote from the current evidence alone.
The authorized next work is only an evidence-package preflight. This still does
not authorize actual promotion, deployment, training, replay, candidate
generation, DP changes, online selector changes, or safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

## Current V14 Trained Shadow Replay/Evaluation Promotion Evidence-Package Preflight Ready After 9aea47

The promotion evidence-package preflight was executed on AutoDL after the
promotion-decision plan authorized this preflight-only gate. The gate consumed
only existing promotion-decision, result-review, training-review, training
summary, weights, atom scales, and shadow execution hash artifacts. It did not
promote, deploy, train CAMP, run replay, generate candidates, modify DP, change
online selection, or make safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP claims.

The first attempt is retained as evidence. It rejected because the checker
treated `dp_native_training_data_contract` as a scalar string, while the actual
training artifact stores the validated contract as a dictionary with schema
`clean_dp_native_training_data_contract_validator_v1` and 32 selection-log
members. Commit `9aea47cc48aad4be26d8221e3c6c40dcf612d9d1` corrected the
static contract check and the rerun passed.

The decision is still conservative: this is not promotion. The authorized next
work is only a default-off shadow selector static integration contract plan.
Executed trajectory selection remains DP Top-1 until a separate promotion gate.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Static Integration Contract Plan Ready After 8fe12a

The default-off shadow selector static integration contract plan was executed
on AutoDL after the promotion evidence-package preflight authorized this
plan-only gate. The gate read the evidence-package preflight plus current CAMP
selector, replay runner, and Benders atom contract test surfaces. It did not
implement selector wiring, promote, deploy, train CAMP, run replay, generate
candidates, modify DP, change online selection, or make safety-benefit/CAMP-over-DP
claims.

The plan pins a conservative static contract: any future implementation must
remain default-off, fail closed to DP Top-1, log only the shadow selected index,
use the fixed current-tick DP candidate tensor, compute affine
`score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, and keep executed trajectory output on DP Top-1 until a
separate promotion gate.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Implementation Plan Ready After 55c360

The default-off shadow selector implementation plan-only gate was added,
tested locally, pushed, synchronized to AutoDL, and executed against the
previous static integration contract plan artifact. This gate only plans a
future implementation. It does not edit production selector wiring, train CAMP,
run replay, generate candidates, modify DP, promote atoms or selectors, deploy,
or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

The first AutoDL execution used the static-contract artifact root instead of
the `report/` JSON path and was correctly rejected with
`source_static_contract_plan_failure`. The successful rerun used the
`report/default_off_shadow_selector_static_integration_contract_plan.json`
source JSON from the same prior artifact.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Implementation Unit Tests Passed After 154663

The default-off shadow selector implementation unit-tests-only gate added the
planned focused tests and verified them locally and on AutoDL. This gate wrote
tests only. It did not edit production selector wiring, train CAMP, run replay,
generate candidates, modify DP, promote atoms or selectors, deploy, or
authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

Two initial AutoDL execution attempts are retained as failed environment
artifacts. The first failed because `python3.12` was not on the non-interactive
SSH PATH. The second failed because `/root/miniconda3/bin/python` had no
pytest. The successful rerun used the existing
`/root/autodl-tmp/dp312_venv/bin/python` Python 3.12 environment.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Plan Ready After 2456037

The default-off shadow selector runtime artifact manifest plan-only gate read
the v14 training outputs, the post-implementation static review report, the
implementation result, the replay runner source, current status, and this v14
audit EOF. It planned the future runtime artifact manifest contract for the
default-off shadow selector without writing that runtime manifest.

This gate is plan-only. It did not materialize the runtime manifest, run replay,
train CAMP, generate candidates, modify DP, promote atoms or selectors, deploy,
or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims. The future runtime manifest static
review must prove this plan is not itself a runtime manifest and that the future
manifest would remain default-off, fail-closed, and DP Top-1 for executed
trajectory output.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Static Review Passed After 11f1f7

The runtime artifact manifest static contract review read the successful v14
runtime artifact manifest plan, the current plan script and test, the replay
runner, current status, and this v14 audit EOF. It verified that the source
plan is not itself a runtime manifest, the planned runtime manifest path remains
unmaterialized, the future runtime manifest entries are limited to
`atom_scales` and `static_weights`, the v14 runtime schema/source scope are
pinned, and default-off fail-closed execution remains DP Top-1.

The first AutoDL static-review attempt failed because the static text check
required split Python string literals to appear as one contiguous string. The
remediation relaxed only that source-surface check to require the schema/target
components, added a current-repo source-surface test, and reran successfully.

This gate is static-review-only. It did not materialize the runtime manifest,
run replay, train CAMP, generate candidates, modify DP, promote atoms or
selectors, deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims. It authorizes only
a future materialization plan-only gate, not materialization itself.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Materialization Plan Ready After ddce7a

The runtime artifact manifest materialization plan read the successful v14
runtime artifact manifest static review, the original runtime artifact manifest
plan, the replay runner, current status, and this v14 audit EOF. It planned the
future runtime manifest content for the existing `atom_scales` and
`static_weights` artifacts, including logical/path/basename SHA aliases, but it
did not write the runtime manifest file.

The planned runtime manifest path remains absent after this gate. This gate did
not run replay, train CAMP, generate candidates, modify DP, promote atoms or
selectors, deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims. It authorizes only
future materialization static contract review, not materialization itself.

The runtime artifact manifest materialization static contract review read the
successful materialization plan, current plan script and test, the replay
runner, current status, and this v14 audit EOF. It verified that the source
materialization plan is not a runtime manifest, the planned manifest path
remains absent, future runtime entries remain limited to `atom_scales` and
`static_weights`, and the future manifest keeps the v14 runtime schema/source
scope, fixed K=8 candidate reranking, 9 approved atoms, affine scoring, and DP
Top-1 execution policy pinned.

deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims. It authorizes only a future
materialization implementation-plan gate, not implementation or materialization.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Materialization Implementation Static Review Passed After af4064

The runtime artifact manifest materialization implementation static-contract
review gate read the source implementation plan, the source plan script and
tests, current status, and this v14 audit EOF. The first run rejected the gate
because the source-surface checks were too brittle: they expected full long
authorization constants in contiguous source text, while the source plan script
used adjacent string literals. The remediation changed those checks to contract
suffix checks, then reran the same static-review gate successfully.

This gate is static review only. It did not implement or run the future
materializer, did not write or materialize the runtime manifest, did not run
replay, did not train CAMP, did not generate candidates, did not modify DP, did
not promote atoms or selectors, did not deploy, and did not authorize
safety/CAMP-over-DP claims. It authorizes only the future materializer
implementation gate.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Materializer Implementation Complete After 9b772d

The materializer implementation gate added the default-off/fail-closed runtime
artifact manifest builder and focused tests. The builder remains inert unless
the explicit materializer flag is provided. When enabled, it verifies the source
implementation-plan SHA256, fixed DP head, planned output path, runtime schema,
default-off/fail-closed/static-selector contract, `score_k(w)=a_k^T w`, and the
`atom_scales`/`static_weights` artifact hashes before writing exactly one
manifest via same-directory temp file plus atomic replace.

This gate did not materialize the real planned runtime manifest, run replay,
train CAMP, generate candidates, modify DP, promote atoms or selectors, deploy,
or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims. It authorizes only the next
post-implementation static contract review gate.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Materializer Post-Implementation Static Review Passed After 97754f

The runtime artifact manifest materializer post-implementation static-contract
review gate read the source implementation plan, materializer implementation,
materializer tests, current status, and this v14 audit EOF. The first run
rejected the gate because two source-surface checks expected full long schema
constants in contiguous source text, while the materializer uses adjacent string
literals. The remediation changed those checks to schema suffix checks, then
reran the same static-review gate successfully.

This gate is static review only. It did not materialize the real runtime
manifest, run replay, train CAMP, generate candidates, modify DP, promote
selectors or atoms, deploy, or authorize runtime execution or safety/CAMP-over-DP
claims. It authorizes only the next runtime artifact manifest materialization
gate.

## Current V14 Default-Off Shadow Selector Runtime Artifact Manifest Materialized After bae519

The runtime artifact manifest materialization gate executed the reviewed
materializer once against the approved source implementation plan. The planned
runtime manifest path did not exist before the run, and the materializer wrote
exactly the planned JSON manifest. The materialized manifest is default-off,
fail-closed, static-selector metadata for CAMP shadow reranking of fixed DP
candidate tensors; it does not execute the selector or alter DP Top-1 output.

This gate did not run replay, train CAMP, generate candidates, modify DP,
promote selectors or atoms, deploy, or authorize safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.
It authorizes only the next default-off selector runtime shadow replay preflight
gate.

This gate is read-only evidence packaging. It consumed immutable source
artifacts and hashes from the passed runtime promotion-decision plan, runtime
result review, shadow-vs-Top1 static delta review, runtime manifest, training
artifact static review, training summary, weights/scales, and runtime shadow
execution SHA256SUMS. It did not train, replay, generate candidates, modify DP,
change the online selector, promote atoms or selectors, deploy, or authorize
safety/CAMP-over-DP claims.

The preflight only authorizes a future evidence-package static review. CAMP is
still bounded to shadow reranking/selecting fixed DP candidate tensors by
affine `score_k(w)=a_k^T w` over approved atoms with nonnegative simplex
weights. Executed trajectory selection remains DP Top-1.
